Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The structures operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 776. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 583 with 3 eigenvalues on the boundary.

At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 583. QED.
